TrueHue is a Python ML pipeline (thesis project) for smartphone-based skin-tone correction. Pipeline: face detection â†’ skin mask â†’ ITA â†’ MST â†’ decision-tree correction â†’ corrected image.

## Commands (run from project root)

- `venv\Scripts\activate` then `pip install -r requirements.txt` â€” Python env lives in `venv/` (Windows, Python 3.14; `requires-python` is >=3.10 but the actual env is 3.14).
- `pytest tests/ -v` â€” run the test suite. No linter or typechecker is configured; pytest is the only verification step.
- `python scripts/run_hello_world.py` â€” environment sanity check.

## Import convention

All package imports use the `src.` root namespace: `from src.utilities.config import get_config`, `from src.ita_estimation.compute_ita import compute_ita`.

- Always run scripts and pytest from the **project root** â€” `src.*` imports resolve only because `src/` is importable from cwd (pyproject sets `pythonpath = ["."]` for pytest; scripts require same cwd).
- The distributable name is `truehue` but code imports `src.*`, not `truehue.*`.

## Current state (important â€” don't assume features exist)

## Structure & conventions

31 October2026 expert-feedback clarification: after a good initial correction
model exists, the user adopts professional review through the existing Google
Form, feedback-informed model refinement and professional re-review. This
matches the proposal's testing/refinement phase. Earlier "after completion"
wording must not be interpreted as banning refinement after an initial review.
Keep development feedback rounds/versioned outputs separate from a frozen
final appraisal; do not fabricate responses or contact professionals without
instructions. See `docs/expert-feedback-refinement-policy.md`.

User clarification (28 October 2026): photographer opinions are needed only after the correction model is finished, using the user's existing Google Form. The locally generated photographer forms are unused; do not treat collecting their responses as a model-development prerequisite. Prioritize correction-model improvement.

- Central config: `src/utilities/config.py` (`Config` dataclass) â€” holds data/model/output paths, `IMAGE_SIZE`, and the `MST_LABELS` (10 Monk Skin Tone labels 0â€“9). Reuse these constants instead of hardcoding paths/labels.
- `data/`, `models/`, `outputs/` subdirs are fully gitignored (only `.gitkeep` files tracked). Do not commit raw datasets or model binaries.
- MST-E, FairFace, SCIN datasets are extern/not in-repo â€” never upload raw datasets.
- The ML polymer output targets on-device Android, but all current work is Python.

## Branching (from README)

- `main` = stable/demonstrable only; `develop` = WIP; `ml-pipeline`, `android-ui`, `testing-documentation` = feature branches. Don't commit to `main` for work-in-progress.

## Docs

`docs/` holds `implementation-plan.md` (fixed scope: Android, on-device, single face; model = decision-tree regression of gamma/red gain/blue gain), `setup-guide.md`, `decision-log.md`, `expirement-log.md`. Read `implementation-plan.md` before designing new pipeline stages.
